ACW — Card

Courage 130, 9 December 2010

9 December 2010
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
A C W THE EMPIRE STRIKES BACK COURAGE 130 ¤ 09 DECEMBER 2010 LAND ROVER ARENA, BOLOGNA, ITALY INTRODUCTION PRE-SHOW ¦ HOUR ONE ¦ HOUR TWO Introduction At END GAME 2010, 30 All-Stars (inclusive of some surprise guests) will do battle for bragging rights and a guaranteed shot at the ACW World Championship at the biggest pay-per-view on the ACW calendar. But right now, many of these All-Stars are finding it hard to concentrate on what is an opportunity of a lifetime. Keith Scott Zimmerman's got one eye on the raging battle for ACW's survival, and the other eye on his challenger -- who just so happens to have Keith's title belt in his possession. Christopher Sheffield is bracing himself to be SilverHAWK's trump card against Hunt's forces (as the Man Without An Alias once was when Ethan Winters ran roughsod way back when)... but finds himself being threatened by Fejona Min, who dropped a heck of a bombshell last week. To say that ACW resembles a war-torn battleground would be an understatement. SilverHAWK will also be the first to say that this isn't the first time he's had to dance this dance. Hell, it ain't even the fourth time. But despite his son GoldenHAWK and various other personalities rallying together for the ACW cause, there's a large amount of fear gnawing away at HAWK's insides. Because this time, when he sees Jeremy Hunt and God's Forgotten Son standing side by side, unwavering in their single-minded belief of *their* cause? ... It makes SilverHAWK think that the end times are truly here. Next: PRE-SHOW. ---- IN THE DARK~! IN THE DARK~! #1: Carrachio Salfuego, JP Boudreau & Quintana Rogerio Santiago vs. MIS, Koji Takada & 'The Natural' Nathan Adams Three fan favourites versus three individuals the fans didn't care for. Fine way to kick off the night, many fans thought. In this explosive Six-Men Tag Team Texas Tornado Match, the crowd were hoping for a change in fortunes for Carrachio Salfuego... as well as another chance to see JP Boudreau in action, and the rising Quintana Rogerio Santiago possibly pick up another win. Koji Taka and Nathan Adams, formerly at loggerheads, were now seemingly on the same page with each other. They were joined by MIS, the Japanese Wrecking Ball who had earned an upset win over Salfuego the previous week thanks to the interference of 'The Two' Booster Reid. Despite being heavily jeered; the trio of MIS, Takada and Nathan Adams had only one mission. Win. And they took the fight to their opposing combatants early on. Seeing how it was being competed under Texas Tornado rules, there was mayhem all over. Koji and JP had paired off and taken their brawl to ringside. Nathan Adams was being schooled by Quintana on the stage, leaving MIS and Carrachio Salfuego to do battle in the ring. This time 'round, Salfuego didn't leave anything to chance and bombarded MIS with everything in arsenal. Even as MIS threatened to claw his way back into the contest, the return of Quintana to the ring (having handily disposed of the miserable Nathan Adams on the stage) turned the tide back to Salfuego's favour. And the two teamed up to great effect. Quintana took MIS out with a release German suplex, giving Salfuego room to connect with a scintillating 450° splash off the top of the corner turnbuckle. The three-count was never in doubt after that. Post-match, JP re-joined his fellow compatriots in the ring for a quick celebration... but the sight of 'The Two' Booster Reid, on the stage scowling at Carrachio, put a stop to all that. Carrachio and Booster locked eyes, suggesting that whatever rivalry was stirring between the two of them was far from over. IN THE DARK~! #2: Bobby Minio vs. Fight Man The fans were in a treat for this next match-up. Why, you ask? For one, it was to be competed under Scorpion Fighting Rules. Secondly and perhaps most important of all? Fight Man was involved. Despite being somewhat new, Fight Man garnered a scary amount of cheers en route to the ring. He was the beloved Hardcore Hero of the people, and protector of Botham City. Wherever the hell that is. In any case, Fight Man was raring to fight and he smiled widely when his opponent was revealed to be none other that Bobby Minio. Minio, a reluctant member of Z's evil super-group (i.e. the cabal) had found himself out of matches recently. His last meaningful on-screen appearance was that of a waiter, serving champagne to fellow Cabalite Kati on COURAGE 128. Needless to say, Minio was glad for the chance to fight; and in this environment, against Fight Man? Bobby was thrilled. What followed was a rather awesome battle. Fight Man and Bobby Minio left their inhibitions in the locker-room and went to down on each other. At one point, Minio was angling to powerbomb Fight Man through the Pakistani Broadcast Booth, drawing ire from the Pakistani Broadcast Correspondents (Akbar and Feroz). Fight Man countered at the last second via a back body drop, a move which saw Minio's head graze the edge of the Pakistani Broadcast Booth. The fight raged on after that, with all eyes on the 10-minute time-limit. Finally, with nine minutes and thirty seconds elapsed, Bobby Minio triumphed; he had levelled Fight Man with his One Armed Scissor signature move onto a steel chair! It was enough to keep the Hardcore Hero down for the win, but Minio appeared to have garnered respect for his fallen adversary in the process. IN THE DARK~! #3: Dolf Biggler vs. Valley Baptiste The last time Valley Baptiste was in a match, it was on COURAGE 126. He and his rival MIS got crushed by Rory Hayes in an IN THE DARK~! match-up. Valley had suitably recovered from being beaten badly by Hayes, and now wanted to end the year on a high note. Valley had been labelled as one to watch since debuting in the All-Star Academy earlier in the year, but he hadn't fulfilled his potential. His opponent, on the other hand? Well, Dolf Biggler had the unfortunate honour of squaring off against Fejona Min in his debut last week. Dolf lost. Badly. Tapped out, and did so while crying like a little girl. A fact the fans were only too quick to remind Biggler of. Dolf commanded the fans to remain silent as he tried to explain his journey to ACW. Neither the fans nor Valley Baptiste cared much about listening to Dolf's origin story. So, Baptiste smacked Biggler in the face with a high knee to get the match under way. The fans loved that. What the fans didn't love, was when the two men spilled to the outside; because that allowed Dolf to turn the tables. Biggler did so by connecting with a drop toe hold on Baptiste that saw the latter's face crash into the edge of the ring apron. Hello, busted nose! Dolf, proving that he was an opportunist at heart, managed to manuever Valley back into the ring and scored dramatically with a reverse bulldog. That proved to be enough to keep Valley Baptiste down for the three-count. An impressive turn of events for Dolf Biggler, racking up his first victory. Too bad about the fans sticking it to him. "CRYBABY~!" chants rang out loudly, causing Biggler to storm off from ringside in a fit. IN THE DARK~! #4: Big E. Smalls vs. Dinamo Illyich The next match would definitely be important to the victors as recently, both teams had fallen under hard times. Dinamo Illyich made their way to the ring, the fans showing a little bit of love for Pukazho and Jeinmenner, the lovable brawlers from the Ukraine. While their won-loss record wasn’t quite the best, the fans loved them for their never-say-die attitudes. Big E. Smalls followed suit, this being the first time Big E. Stark would compete in a match since COURAGE 127 when he got put through a glass table. Mach 2 started the match off fast and furious like his namesake against the bigger Pukazho. Dizzying him with an array of arm drags and headscissors takedowns, it didn’t take too long for Mach 2 to bust out some high-octane offense, flying to the outside with a somersault tope con hilo. Giving some fans high-fives after his dive, he took Pukazho back into the ring, but a pair of inverted atomic drops stunned him long enough to bring in Jeinmenner. Jeinmenner and Pukazho took turns teeing off on the smaller member of Big E. Smalls, making quick tags for several minutes and throwing blows into the rib cage of Mach 2. A big splash form Jeinmenner missed, but the CCS Enzuigiri from Mach 2 did not. He followed it up with what could only be described as a backwards victory roll into a tornado DDT on his much bigger opponent. The tag was made to Big E. Stark, who looked pretty good for a guy who ate glass a few weeks prior. He bowled over both members of Dinamo Illyich, stacking them up across the ring before hitting a running hip attack. A reverse thrown DVD on Jeinmenner got a close two, but Pukazho broke the fall. Jeinmenner reversed and hit a spinebuster of his own on Big E. for two. With all four men in the ring, Pukazho tried disposing of Mach 2, but a headscissors sent him out to the floor. Big E. grabbed Jeinmenner by the head in a Cutter position, allowing Mach 2 to crack him in the back of the head before dropping him with the cutter! The combination they called The Top Gun scored the three and Big E. Smalls scored an impressive win as a duo. Big things were clearly on the horizon for this young go-getting team. IN THE DARK~! #5: Der Deutscher Soldat vs. Carl Kimball And for the final IN THE DARK~! match-up for the evening, the fans had the pleasure of welcoming everybody's favourite married tag team -- Monte Young & Carl Kimball. Union O' MonteCarl, as they had been called ever since being signed to ACW contracts. On this night, Carl Kimball would find himself in singles action, and Monte Young was relegated to cheering on his partner from ringside. Before the introduction of his opponent, Carl had the crowd in stitches by blowing kisses to Monte on the outside. Needless to say, Monte was rather embarrassed. Once the husky Der Deutscher Soldat hit the scene, Carl Kimball tossed aside all the tomfoolery. Now under the tutelage of the nefarious Z in the cabal, Soldat looked meaner and much more volatile. Evidence of that was on display last week, when DDS and Ryan Billows took down Midnight Cowboy and Jonny Yankee. Hence, it was probably wise that Kimball adopted a hit-and-run type of attack. The trouble with that was, Soldat never let Kimball run too far... and after two minutes of frenzied brawling, DDS finally brought down the hammer; gorilla press slam into a backbreaker. Across his knee! Monte Young thought his partner had been broken into half. It was a fair assumption, for Kimball never quite recovered from that. After throwing his opponent around for a while more like a rag doll, Soldat connected with a fierce leg-drop off the ropes and the subsequent cover yielded the undeniable three-count. Another impressive outing for DDS as a newly-minted foot soldier of the cabal. As for Kimball? He was duly tended to by Monte Young, who found that Kimball demanded for kisses to his forehead to 'make the owwies go away'. Again, with the crowd egging him on, Monte Young had little choice but to do so. You can imagine how deliriously over the moon Kimball was with his partner's public display of affection. fight for freedom "I want my friend back. Now!" Those were the rehearsed and demanding words of referee Trent Savage. Yes, you read correctly. Who was he actually talking to? Let's play the guessing game. ... Are those protests I hear? Screw you. Clue #1: He wears a fuschia mask. Clue #2: He's a demented super-villain. Clue #3: He has, at his disposal, many men and women to fight his battles. You've got it, people. "Hh. NO." Z fired back, all cheeky like. The Masked Enigma looked around the hallway he was in, and was tickled by the number of glares he was receiving from various members of the staffing committee. Trent Savage fumed, and refused to budge from his spot. He had been deliberating over whether or not he should approach Z with regards to bartering for the freedom of his friend, Pete Strombo. Watching Z swagger into the Land Rover Arena without a care in the world made Trent's mind up, and he decided to corner Z. After all, what else could he lose, right? "I've had enough of you and your dickish ways, Z!" Trent raised his voice, snapping Z out of his daydreams involving hot Italian super-models bending over for him. "You orchestrated some grand scheme to dupe Pete, and you've held him captive for a couple of months now. That's not right. I don't care what plans you have for ACW. I just want my friend back. I've done nothing wrong to see my friend being used as your slave. That's why I have a deal for you. I have somebody who's just as tired of you as I am. If he beats you -- and I know he will -- I get Pete back. I think that's fair! What do you say, ZED?" Trent Savage stuck his chest out, proud of his man-up moment. It was exhilirating standing up to the Dastardly Mastermind, but the moment soon passed. Especially as Z stopped smirking. Narrowing his eyes, Z placed his hands on his hips. "Alright, then. You've got my attention, you little snot. First off: you've done nothing wrong? That's debatable, but a story best saved for another times. Perhaps when you're not so busy getting a boner over the forbidden fruit that is Paige Allen. Yeah, I know about your crush on Paige. Everybody knows, you filthy pussy-hound. Anyways. Interesting deal you've got there. I actually already HAVE a match scheduled. Rory Hayes is going to pay for his sins. You know what, though? I'm actually very intrigued by your proposal. However. It's a bit one-sided. What happens in the event that your mysterious trump card fails against me? Gloating about a victory and getting to hold on to ol' Pete Strombo ain't enough for me. Hence, I have a counter-proposal for you. Your guy wins, you get Pete Strombo back and you can go ahead to celebrate the night with Paige Allen. Plunder her beautiful booty. I know that's what you have planned. For shame. Now, if I win? Or rather... WHEN my hand is raised in victory? Hh. ... You join the cabal as my newest recruit." Trent Savage's jaw hit the floor. Like, literally. At first, during Z's tirade of a reply, Trent was disgusted at the obscene remarks about Paige. While it was true that ol' TS had a major crush on Paige, he could never quite work up the courage (hey!) to pull the trigger and reveal his feelings for her. Therefore, Trent figured he and Paige would just remain as friends. Besides, Paige was still smarting over the disappearance of the love of her life. But after Z spat out his idea of a counter-proposal? All Trent could think of was life as a Cabalite? Still, the referee had a feeling that Z's luck was about to run out. The person that Trent had lined up would definitely do the trick. "In more ways than one." Trent mumbled to himself. Z tilted his head downwards. "What's that, now?" Trent shook his head and looked up at the evil eyes of the Caped Crusader. "Nothing. Yeah, you know what? Fine. If that's the deal that's going to make you happy, I'm game. Because you're going to lose. You're finally going to get what's coming to you, and you will be forced to give back Strombo to me. Let me know when during the show you want to get your teeth kicked in, and I'll bring out my mystery guest. I can't wait for the moment when you see who it is. See you later, ZED." Spinning on his heels, Trent stormed down the hallway. He'd begun second-guessing his decision to accept Z's termss and conditions, but what's done was done. The popular zebra (i.e. referee) began cooking up visions of Z's defeat later in the evening, and hoped that his friend Pete Strombo was a-okay. Z? He put his gloved hands together and rubbed them. Like a Bond villain would. "Tonight's going to be bloody interesting." Next: HOUR ONE. ---- Everyone Here? Good... COURAGE 130 would kick off in a slightly different manner than usual. “The war is over, and we have won.” With a cold open, it appeared. Speaking of cold; the words that escaped from Jeremy Hunt’s lips were downright frosty, but the SlySports Programming Executive really did believe his own words. I mean, the jeers of the Italian crowd in the Land Rover Arena put it bluntly how much they hated that idea… though they didn’t know what Keller winning last week and Sheffield not even being able to truly face him, meant as far as THE BATTLE FOR ACW was concerned. The tired, slightly aged, yet ever posh Englishman simply smiled though. He believed he knew the answer. Hell, Hunt believed he had the answer all along. Arrogant sone of a bitch. “Last week, the Huntsmen’s own Khristain Keller defeated YOUR World Champion, Keith Scott Zimmerman!” Hunt said with that chilling smile as K2 postured behind him with the stolen WORLD CHAMPIONSHIP over his shoulder, the rest of the Huntsmen there with him, “Yes, the very man that Mr. Keller will face at END GAME 2010, this time for the ACW World Championship. And then, once Mr. Zimmerman was able to twist our contender into a confrontation with Christopher Sheffield… the match never even began, and the one who first stole All-Star Championship from the esteemed Ethan Winters Sr.--” God’s Forgotten Son could be seen shaking his head in disgust, Spirit Of ACW Championship over his shoulder, behind Hunt as the Bologna crowd continued to jeer. “--was taken off the table.” The jeers grew louder; I mean The Pulp Hero didn’t even get a chance at a match! “Some say it was only possible because of help from the cabal!” The crowd quieted, I mean wouldn’t an alliance between these two factions be the absolute worst case scenario, “But it is my belief that Ms. Min had her own agenda in going after Mr. Sheffield… and in doing so acted outside of the cabal. Either way, it is ALSO my belief that Mr. ZED and I after two different things. It seems he wants the world… and he can have that, because all I have ever wanted?” Hunt then turned and motioned to God’s Forgotten Son, to a contemptful look from the King Shit of Fuck Mountain. “All WE have ever wanted is All-Star Championship Wrestling. It is our birthright.” The crowd jeered, but Hunt could only smile that chilling smile. “And now we have it.” TICK, TICK, BOOM! The Hives put the posturing of the still-far-from-fully-in-control Jeremy Hunt to a stop, and not only was the World Champion Keith Scott Zimmerman making his way out to the top of the ramp, but ACW General Manager SilverHAWK and his son GoldenHAWK was with them. As was The Man Without an Alias, Christopher Sheffield. And with him, the extremely battered (by the cabal) but not broken tag team of Big E. Smalls, Carrachio Salfuego, and Orlando Grant. The ACW GM took a deep breath and then turned his head to Hunt in the ring, his eyes ablaze with balls of fury. “How dare you come out here and run your mouth like this! Winters’ Forgotten Son might be the one holding the Spirit Of ACW, but that doesn’t give any of you bastards an inch in this battle!” HAWK roared, drawing an immense eruption of cheers from the crowd. Hunt rolled his eyes. But it was clear that HAWK was not done. "ACW is about RESPECT, it's about HONOUR! It's NOT about manipulating others, it's not about this GANG WARFARE, bullshit! We were done that when we got rid of Ethan Winters the FIRST TIME!" And just like that, Keith Scott Zimmerman had the mic. And he damn sure wasn't going to let what sounded like a dictionary definition standing ovation stop him. "I have one simple question for the lot of you standing in the HAWK'S ring..." Another raucuous cheer from the Italians in attendance. "How stupid ARE you?" RUAHH~! Hunt looked highly offended, GFS glared, & Keller merely held up the stolen World Championship in response. "Do you not know who I am? Do you not think I have a Plan B? Do you think the series of events that actually brought Sheffield and I TOGETHER can end any single fucking way but us raining down destruction on you simple-minded taintsniffers!?" This just in: Keith Scott Zimmerman is displeased. "You may've won the day last week, but Keller is just playing pretend. You want to walk around after a sneak attack and fluke rollup with the leather and gold, you enjoy it, Baldy. I mean, get all the pictures you can, take a couple of YouTube videos with it, to be honest I don't give a damn if Hunt puts it on your bald head while he fires your pay down your throat while you're on your knees--" Chris Sheffield looked down, his shoulders moving up and down in a north/south motion. SilverHAWK couldn't hide his own laughter at that while the Huntsmen flipped out and Jeremy Hunt had to restrain GFS from making a charge up the aisle. "...because END GAME is called that for a reason, you gaggle of idiots, and at END GAME it's going to be the end of the SlySports virus, the end of King Shit walking around with MY title, and the end of that walking abortion ad walking around with the Spirit. Oh, believe it, you bunch of easily led sheep: at END GAME, the universe goes back to unfolding like it should, and I get back MY ACW WORLD CHAMPIONSHIP!" Can I get a witness from the congregation!? Just as the fans thought it couldn't get more intense, they were rather abruptly joined by several individuals representing a diabolical man who evidently had his own agenda. "Well, well. We've got a pissing contest here, it seems!" were the scathing words of one Fejona Min, flanked on either side by Jack Harris and Kesavan. Der Deutscher Soldat was in front of her, shielding the Rogue Slayer from any frontal attacks, while Bobby Minio had the rear (and a nice view of Fejona's rear it was HAHA SCORE) covered. On the stage, Sheffield seethed. In the ring, the likes of Keller and Abilities blew a gasket. Fejona Min? She just smirked it up, while keeping a cautionary eye on the fans in the vicinity. The sight of Harris barking at the fans and making some of them pee their pants comforted Fejona. "Evening, everybody. Z couldn't be here for this little talkity talk, so I'm representing him. Except, I have nothing to say as far as ACW's survial is concerned. Z and Z alone knows what he wants to do with ACW. Jeremy Hunt, I have to correct you on two counts. One; he doesn't WANT the world. If he did, he'd already have it. And two; I absolutely acted in the best interests of the cabal last week when I blindsided Sheffield. Although, yes, there's a element of personal drama attached to it. Can you really blame me, though? The Man Without An Alias here is family, after all. And those who know me and my past, will know that I have a problem with family. I wonder how my cousin's doing now. *tt* Irrelevant at this point, let's get back on track here since I don't think our show lasts six hours. So, Christopher. Have you been able to garner the significance of my mother being in the photo yet? Hmm? Or are you still drawing blanks?" Fejona lowered the microphone and half-scowled at Sheffield. Keith turned to the Analogue Man, as if to ask 'want me to run over there and kick her in the teeth'... but the former Alias just remained rooted to the spot, eyes burning in rage. Jeremy Hunt actually allowed himself to smile a tiny bit. This was interesting. “Y'know, if you're still struggling to piece everything together, I think I might help!" Fejona spoke again, her tone taking a decidedly wicked twist. "I’ve been talking to someone else who knows a thing or two about the picture you hold. He’s a big fan of Z’s and also a big fan of yours still, Sheff. Very big fan. Giant. I would almost say he's obsessed about you. He said something to me about how it was good that you didn’t die young after all, and he’d love to have a warm-up match before he enters himself into the 2010 End Game Match coming up in several weeks. What say you? I can give him a call after this and set it up if you're game.” The Original Pulp Hero had been handed the microphone from the Only CHAMPION That Matters. He had chosen his words, and didn't even give a damn about Fejona's latest bit of information. “You wanna give me a mystery opponent?” Sheffield growled, as he paced back and forth on the stage. “You wanna pick my poison? How ‘bout I return the favor?! See, after what you said to me about legacy and about family… it hit me that I hadn’t touched base with a person that had helped me return to my wrestling legacy.” The Italian crowd was excited, while also happy to acknowledge the comeback of Alias, when ACW returned in 2009. “And this someone, well they’re the closest thing I’ve had to family in a good long while, outside of the ACW faithful… and though I’ve lost touch with ‘em, I’m more then happy to have ‘em around just in time to kick your fuckin’ face in!” The crowd roared, as Fejona Min sneered. Keller, in the ring, wasn’t going to be left without saying a word however; I mean that’d be sacrilegious. “Speaking of getting your fuckin’ face kicked in… it’s a shame you’re match didn’t go as planned last week, hey Sheff?” Abe Swift chuckled through the gauze that KSZ had left him with after dusting his chin with brass-knucks, and nudged his Abilities confidant, William T. Rex. “That’s alright though, we’ve got this week covered like my dick on Elixr’s lips.” The King Shit couldn’t help but shift his attention to the mouth of Abilities, but before he could say anything a commotion could be seen from the other side of the crowd. “WHOA, WHOA, WHOA, WHOA, WHOA, WHOA…BITCHES, LIKE, WHOA!” From the other side came none other than the six members of ACW’s greatest (only?) raver contingent, The Night Life. All six members stood in attendance, Tyson XL carrying his [Black] Scorpion Championship while The Amazing Gabriel led the pack, carrying both ACW Tag Team title belts. Kaus, Fever Pitch, Zip Zap, and Elixr came “You guys can’t POSSIBLY be engaging in a dick-measuring contest without the Party-Starter himself showing up to make idiots out of all of you… ‘cept Abe Swift, he does that every time he opens his Bill Cosby-looking mushmouth. And Elixr’s lips are for pleasuring my Texas-sized penis and nobody else’s!” She smiled as the fans continued to boo. Abe Swift shouted a few more words that would have American censors scrambling for the red button. “The Caballsacks can sit there and count down time on a clock, Hunt's Dirty Vagina Lips Brigade can sit there and spout mummy dust from said vajeen and Team ACW, while we’re allies in name only… that’s it. What you’re all not seeing here is the big picture here… one of US is winning that End Game Match and we’ll be more than happy to put the ACW World Championship around my sexy-ass waist! And with the Four Ways To Glory, I’ll be happy to deal with SlySports Mystery Opponents Tee-Em, then we’ll be showing the Inbreed Gang, Lack of Abilities, and the jacked-up Cabal nimrods just why we ARE the ACW Tag Team Champions!” The Payne Gang, recent qualifiers for the Four Ways To Glory Match at END GAME 2010 stomped out from the back and joined SilverHAWK and his supporters on the ramp. Just as KC and Kassidy Payne had stood to one side beside Salfuego however, a commotion could be seen as not only did Mr. Power and Mr. Trip jump out of the crowd and stand atop the Broadcast Booth, but their entire Entourage deemed fit to join them. Reginauld Cardinal wasn’t exactly pleased by the unprofessionalism of these two men, but upcoming ACW Hall Of Fame inductee Jimmy Reid was more then happy to allow a member of the Entourage to sit on his lap. PowerTrip weren’t saying anything, but The Payne Gang did have a few harsh words for the current ACW Tag Team Champions, The Night Life. “It’s just a shame you cunts haven’t qualified for the Four Ways To Glory Match, yet!” KC Payne said with a glorious sneer too. “When you say Cunt, you should be looking at the ring, asshole!” Abraham Swift yelled directly at the rivals of Abilities, the Payne Gang, before adding to the displeasure of Mr. Hunt, “But then again, Hunt’s Cunts have alreeeady qualified!” “Quiet Mr. Swift, I will not be interrupted any further!” “I’ll still wondering what the fuck honest Abe here was talkin’ about when he said that they had shit ‘covered this week’,” was all that Keller could growl towards Jeremy Hunt. “Well tonight, in the final leg of THE BATTLE FOR ACW before END GAME 2010, God’s Forgotten Son and I have decided that since last week ended without a concrete victor… that’ll be going in a different direction,” Keller wasn’t pleased with this, but he wasn’t going to say anything about it right NOW, so Hunt continued, “and be considered one of momentum before God’s Forgotten Son puts the Spirit Of ACW on the line against the top contender…” All eyes suddenly shifted to Christopher Sheffield, who might be forced to take his mind off of Fejona Min, it seemed. “And against said contender, well… ACW’s future will hang in the balance, along with the Spirit Of ACW. But before that, tonight… the team that will walk out of END GAME 2010 with the TAG TEAM CHAMPIONSHIPS… ABILITIES!” Abraham Swift and William T. Rex raised their arms with much aplomb as the Italian crowd jeered, “Will face… well… a tag team…” Hunt looked around at the mass of factions, teams and otherwise, that surrounded the ring and the Huntsmen within it. “Of SilverHAWK’s choice.” SilverHAWK could only scowl, as with but those words from Hunt… EVERY team in the arena save for Abilities had in their minds set that they would be Hawk’s choice. SilverHAWK tried for a moment to settle the mass of All-Stars around him but between the Payne Gang, KSZ and Sheffield, Mach 2, The Night Life, PowerTrip and yes… even Kesavan and Jack Harris, more then a dozen voices where yelling at him to listen to reason and choose THEM. Surrounded by voices, and SilverHAWK left with one very important choice, COURAGE finally switched into its opening sprawl of images and theme song. This was going to be a night to remember, no doubt about it. Follow The Leader With things turning from the general chaos that deemed to start off COURAGE 130, we went to the backstage area, the people out in TV Land found themselves looking upon the quartet of Aria Murphy, Rud Albion, Rhyme and Reason. Part of a group that many might have expected to see out in the ringside area with the rest of the factions if they didn't have more pressing, personal matters to discuss. Albion was no longer the cover boy for GQ, as his tie was loosened around his neck and his sports jacket was crumpled up on the floor. The twins were whispering amongst themselves with a look of concern written all over their faces to which Aria looked over to the pair and shook her head in annoyance. "Why did we come here? This isn't the brightest idea." "Because we love Maddy. That's why." "But these guys are CRAZY. I mean the girl wants to skin people and eat their intestines. I don't know about you, but I'm ninety-five percent sure that I need my intestines." "Shut the f*ck up! How the hell does he deal with you two retards?" Aria asked aloud, which got a look from Rud while the twins scurried to the furthest part of the bench out of fear. "Ah yes... deafness." "Are you all about finished?" Tonight: IT WOULD BE THE REMATCH!!! JONNY YANKEE VS. RYAN BILLOWS! What Shall We Use To Fill The Empty Spaces? Oso Bannon stared blankly out of the tinted window of his rented limousine, the streetlights of Bologna reduced to blurs of white as it weaved through the city’s bustling roads. Pedestrians gawked and whispered at the sight of the vehicle, their minds no doubt filling rapidly with guesses as to who might be inside. The man often known as The Bear (or sometimes just Bear) wondered what they’d say if they knew it was him. He signed as a voice deep inside himself noted that they’d likely be disappointed. Next to him, Chester Wallace’s mouth was moving a mile a minute in between sips from a snifter of pungent, aged brandy that he’d read could only be found in that region of Italy. His demeanor suggested that it had been a necessary pit stop. Usually the epitome of calm and collected, he seemed nervous. Beads of sweat began to collect upon his pale forehead and as the vehicle slowed -- the shadow of the Land Rover Arena growing larger ahead of it -- his mouth maintained speed. And yet his client’s eyes remained where they had been the whole ride from the hotel, Bose earbuds tucked snugly in his ear. Perhaps it was the constant painkiller reduced haze, but Bear had been on a major Pink Floyd kick and on this night was just embarking on his umpteenth journey towards tearing down The Wall. He spared Wallace a glance the agent didn’t even notice and knew whatever he was flapping about was moot as the manifesto of “In the Flesh?” began... “So you thought you might like to go to the show? To feel the warm thrill of confusion... that space cadet glow? Tell me, is something eluding you, sunshine? Is this not what you expected to see? If you want to find out what’s behind these cold eyes, You’ll just have to claw your way through this disguise.” ...For at the end we’ll all be judged. Oso Bannon was just one of the unlucky bastards in this world that has been judged from the start. He had been down and out too many times to count in only twenty two years of life. His only interactions with humanity came in the form of the waddling opportunist behind him, the maniacs on the ACW roster, and the random nobodies who sold him drugs. Otherwise, he was cut off entirely. He’d forgotten how to love himself and so could never love anyone else. He’d been forbidden to do the one thing in life he was ever good at and ended up thrust into doing something he simply couldn’t wrap his head around yet, his recent losses inside the ring still stinging like angry hornets whose nest had been smacked with a stick. His wick was quickly burning at both ends and someone blowing it out before it was too late seemed unlikely... ...Until the week before when Tyson XL leveled him in his rematch against Iceman and extinguished the flame. He had spent the next day in bed self-loathing watching the match on repeat at Wallace’s behest and by the next day his depreciating attitude was replaced with anger. He hit the gym and trained harder than he ever had within the ropes, his always present agent rattling off a list of mistakes he’s made from afar. Yet, when he collapsed exhausted into bed that evening it wasn’t his mistakes that he kept replaying in his mind. Nor was he chasing sleep due to his usual negative self affirming. No... on that evening, he was kept away by something that had long evaded him. Something he could put a face on. At Ohio State, there was no more important game than the last of the season against Michigan. The rivalry was older than some American states and riots occurred annually in the city of Columbus whether or not their Buckeyes won or lost. To say that the whole season wouldn’t mean shit if they lost to the Wolverines would be an understatement and that was a fact that was pounded into men like Oso Bannon for over a half a year leading up to just that one game. The goal was to not look at those Michigan players like peers on the field or even fellow men, but to slaughter them like cattle. “Mr. Bannon?” They were the enemy. And sometimes that’s all you need... “Mr. Bannon?” Oso rubbed his eyes and shook his head, bringing himself back to reality. His mind wandered so easily. He hadn’t even noticed the car stopping or that Chester had already left it. Still seated, he saw the limousine driver looking confused, mouth jabbering away in his direction. He could have turned his music down, but what was the point? He met this man a mere hour earlier and didn’t even remember his name. He reached for the floor of the car and grabbed his trusty duffle bag before stepping out into the dimly lit plaque yellow of yet another random parking structure. Somehow, this one looked so much better than the last. They say, whoever they may be, that one of the many steps you’ll have to climb towards fixing an issue is denial. Stepping inside the arena, he remembered how superior he’d felt to everyone the first time he walked into an All-Star Championship Wrestling event as an employee and how it actually made him sick to his stomach that his mistakes in life had led him there, becoming one of them. Then he recalled for probably the trillionth time the agony of his defeats and the very real physical pain they had caused him. He should have known from the start to give the competition its due. Now, as he walked the halls in search of his dressing room, he waved as he passed some of those very same faces he looked down upon. He began to pick up the pace. Something within him just couldn’t wait to lace up his boots. It was a fire that he thought he’d never feel the burn of so long as he couldn’t be on a football field. His face, as stoic as it was uncharacteristically calm, managed to exude a small smile. For somewhere in the building Big Blue was walking as well, his [Black] Scorpion Championship draped over his shoulder, awaiting the results of a contest which would determine who he’d defend it against at END GAME 2010 (and also awaiting his title defense). Perhaps his season hadn’t gone well so far, but tonight Oso Bannon could get it back on track and earn the right to face his heated, albeit recently acquired rival. A right that he knew if given to him might help him finally get his life back on track as well. Because when you’re a Buckeye, you’re a Buckeye for life and you may have transgressed, but all transgressions are forgiven. So long as you beat the shit out of Michigan... I know he has to be stopped, but not just for the sake of myself and my friends, who he has remorselessly terrorised over the past couple of weeks; not just for the sake of you people, whose lives he endangers with his deranged publicity stunts; but for his OWN sake, too. BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO Green lights, rising fog and the combined hatred of thousands of Italians. After the Bible-burning incident last week, they were frothing at the mouths at the mere sound of Denton's theme music. In four weeks, he had mocked their religion and their way of life, blasphemed his way into the good books of one Jeremy Hunt and turned two of their well-liked heroes into gruesome 'art projects'. So, while the Atheist Militia, his Hunt-sponsored personal escort of eight highly-armed riot cops, dispersed from behind the curtain and lined themselves on either side of the entranceway, trash was thrown, profanities uttered in earnest and “FIRE DEAN DENTON!” chants were proliferated. As the yellow lizard's eyes on the Militia's riot shields greeted the fans with the same calculating gaze that had become a trademark of the Militia's master, the man himself appeared from behind the curtains. His face was shadowed beneath the ripped hood of his battered crocodile-skin waistcoat but a sardonic smile was visible, a glint of sharp silvery teeth between his parted lips. Next: HOUR TWO. ---- SINGLES MATCH - STANDARD RULES Orlando Grant vs. Ryan Billows REFEREE: LEON HURST Originally scheduled to be a guaranteed barn-burner of a rematch, things had changed. Drastically. Jonny Yankee, who would have been looking to prove, once and for all, that he could stand toe to toe with one of, if not the best professional wrestler in the industry, wasn't even present in the arena any longer. Why? Because of a very wicked act put into play by the man he would have been having a rematch against. His horse's leg had been used for batting practice, and now Nellie was in an animal hospital, and Yankee was on his way to be by her side. Family took precedence to everything else, and for The General, Nellie was family. Midnight Cowboy was his very close friend...but Nellie was family... However, speaking of Midnight Cowboy, The Smooth Operator had found a very suitable replacement. Another friend of his, at least as far as playing “Gun” on X-Box 360 while listening to Country music was concerned. And that friend stood in the ring at the moment, working the crowd into a jubilant frenzy. “Yankee's my homie! Big ups to Nellie, I'ma teach that muh'fugga a lesson!” Orlando Grant. And yes, believe it or not, Air Force One was a fan of country. He appreciated it for its simplicity and the attention to the inner struggle of the common man. Kinda deep, huh? Either way, tough luck or good luck, the fans remained firmly behind him. Grant was one of the lucky few who would always have a strong following, simply by being him. Maddox, handcuffed and bludgeoned to a pulp, that truncheon over his head like an executioner's axe... “HEY!” she shouted. As Denton turned, she executed a forward roll and smashed him in the mouth with a lariat from her good arm – or as her mentor called the sequence – the ROLLING CRYSTAL FLASH. The Lizard was staggered by the blow but didn't go down, so she followed up by hitting the ropes and dropkicking him in the knee, before unleashing a brutal buzzsaw kick to the head of the now-kneeling Denton. A SECOND. A THIRD. He wobbled, swayed... and fell FLAT ON HIS FACE to a SCORCHING OVATION. Aria, rather than cover in any traditional fashion, flipped through the air and landed a STANDING shooting star press before hooking the leg with her good arm... ONE TWO THRE – NO. KICKOUT by the Darwinist. She quickly dragged him up to his feet and hooked his head for a DDT, giving the fans a determined nod that said “This is ALL OVER.” But before she could punch a hole in the canvas with the Darwinist's skull, he launched her overhead with a Northern Lights suplex! Rather than hang on to the bridge for the pin, he rolled through until he stood over her, grabbed her arm and pulled her between his legs before rolling onto his back and sinking in a triangle choke. Sorry Aria - an adamantium skeleton can't save you from being CHOKED. THE FUCK. OUT. Aria stood up with Denton's legs wrapped tightly around her neck, reaching with her free arm for the ropes... but he simply grabbed the back of her knee and rolled her further into the middle of the ring. Here, with all of her options for escape expended, he unleashed THE FRONTAL LOBOTOMY. ELBOW after vicious ELBOW rained down on Aria's trapped, strangulated head, each one more forceful than the last. Some fans tried calling her name - she could hear them... further... further away... When he was satisfied with his elbow-based thrashing, he held on to her trapped arm and rolled to the side, transitioning into an omoplata... from here, he simply locked his hands across her face to complete the deadly hold known as THE R-COMPLEX. Aria's injured arm was bent horrifically between Denton's legs and her crushed neckbones were stretched over his knee. The chants, claps and stamps of the fans had no effect. After the armbar, the brainbuster, the triangle choke and the violent elbow strikes... there was nothing left of her. Heinreich called for the bell. BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO While Aria had demonstrated her fire and intensity, Denton's method of cold-bloodedly dismantling her with submissions had proven the perfect strategy. And now, he reaped the rewards: a barrage of trash, boos and chants of “DENTON SUCKS!” Rather than bask in the afterglow of his victory, however, Denton immediately rolled under the bottom rope and grabbed a chair from ringside... WINNER: 'The Darwinist' Dean Denton via SUBMISSION. Weakness ...and as Denton rolled back into the ring with the chair, “Up Jumped the Devil” by Nick Cave cued over the speakers. The piano-based soundtrack to Denton's previous duo of 'masterpieces'; the dissonant, maddening keys that had jangled in the background as Denton stabbed Rud Albion in the face with a scotch glass, as he had threatened to kill Maddox St. James. On the Slytron, the familiar red curtains appeared, swaying with the touch of a gentle breeze. Denton opened the chair out in the middle of the ring. The fans had a feeling something bad was going to happen when they heard that music, but when they saw Arturo DeLillo, the Darwinist's eccentric art agent, jogging down the ramp with a rucksack over his shoulder and a microphone in his hand, they were absolutely certain. The silver-suited, pink-haired ponce climbed the steps and stood by the side of his client as Denton dragged the still largely unconscious Aria to her feet, his hands under her arms, and sat her in the chair. The curtains parted, and three words appeared on the Slytron in the same gilded gold as previous weeks: THE THIRD INSTALMENT “Tonight, Ladies and Gentlemen of Bologna, I am as honoured as you are. Honoured to witness the third, penultimate instalment of my client's transcendent master-works. As visually sensational as they are sensually visceral, as spine-tingling as they are back-breaking, his works have been lauded by critics everywhere for their fierce originality and their transmission of universal truths that we can use to deconstruct and rebuild OUR OWN LIVES!” Arturo finished his speech with a broad, proud smile fixed on his face, as oblivious to the fans' incessant booing and hatred as ever. Meanwhile, Denton opened the rucksack that DeLillo had passed to him and began to pull out... cables? “Using the human body as his medium, Mr. Denton has inspired us with his evolutionary philosophy. His first piece, Trust No One, emphasised the individualistic and self-interested nature of the human race, dismissing our interpersonal relationships as a façade concealing our deeply selfish and cut-throat nature; his second, The Weak Shall Perish, drew attention to the value of natural selection in our modern world, an essential tool which we must use to root out the defective genes of our generation in order to progress! I am sure that I speak for all of us when I ask... what indispensable insight will his THIRD MASTERPIECE bring?” BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO “SHUT – THE – FUCK – UP!” clap-clap-clapclapclap “SHUT – THE – FUCK – UP!” clap-clap-clapclapclap “SHUT – THE – F... HUH?” And the chants were stifled in a flash, as they saw what Denton had pulled out of the bag. It was... a car battery? Attached to the battery was a dial, going up in increments of hundreds of volts, and two jumper cables. Denton swung the clips around in one hand as he paced slowly around Aria, his dead yellow eyes transfixed on her, quizzical and objective like the Lizard of his namesake. Aria was coming to now, Denton's leering face fading in and out of her dream-like view, the sinister piano keys filling her with dread. Arturo held the microphone to Denton's mouth. “Civilisation as we know it is suffering from a great psychological affliction. RELIGION provides a mental breeding ground for all varieties of dysfunction – delusions, fantasies, depression and malaise, rootless guilt... the list goes on. Your natural drives for sex and violence are locked away, suppressed, causing them to manifest in the most perverse of ways... sexual deviancy, binge drinking, drug taking and other self-destructive behaviours. I have tried to save you from yourselves. I have tried to rescue you people from your philosophical exile, your pathological hypocrisy, your self-imposed psychological torture chamber. But the therapeutic value of my art-works has been ignored. Your inbred philistinism has closed off your minds to the degree that I have no other option but to use more extreme measures.” The Lizard stooped down beside Aria and brushed back her hair, then licked out his tongue and grazed it over her ear. The fans almost audibly retched with revulsion as he sucked her earlobe, before clamping the claw of one of the jumper cables to it. “Since the 1930s... Electro-Convulsive Therapy has been used as a last resort to treat mania, schizophrenia and depression, all of which are symptoms of the stress brought on the modern man by his religiosity, his moral façade, his repressed desires...” The Atheist Militia were working hard for their money by this point, holding back the front row with their riot shields and trying their best to contain the outraged fans. After the Bible-burning stunt last week, they didn't need much to be tipped over the edge, and the sight of Denton sucking on Aria's other earlobe and attaching the second jumper cable was enough to incite a bout of trash-throwing so hectic that you might think a flock of birds had become trapped in the arena. Some of it got past the shields and landed in the ring around the semi-conscious Aria and her twisted aggressors, but Denton would not be interrupted. Not while his creative juices were flowing. He stood behind Aria, facing the same direction as her, and balanced the battery on the back of the chair. “Through ECT, I shall purge this woman's mind of its impurities and she will be reduced to her basic drives. I will make an honest woman of her.” The fans' cries for mercy and desperate screams for the She-Wolf were hopeless. Denton traced his finger along the edge of the dial, licking the sweat off his upper lip as he did so, savouring the moment. “STOP!” All eyes turned towards the entranceway. Rud Albion stood, costumed out and ready for war, a sledgehammer in his hands. “STOP THIS DENTON! IT'S NOT TOO LATE TO GO BACK! YOU DON'T HAVE TO DO THIS!” The Atheist Militia formed a barrier of shields in front of the ring, the staring lizard's eyes on their shields all locked on the Bronze Lion as he stood, every muscle tensed, his face tightened into an expression of desperate rage. Denton smiled, ever so slightly. “I told you, Rud. I warned you. Whatever I once was I have sacrificed to my higher purpose, my true calling. I am no longer a just a man, just a kid cheering for Alias in the front row. I am a LIVING EXAMPLE. I am a SAINT. I am a REVOLUTION.” Albion gripped his sledgehammer tighter. “THEN STOP BECAUSE IF YOU DON'T... I SWEAR...” Denton laughed. And turned the dial. “AAAAAAAIIIIIIIIIEEEEEEERRRRGGGGHHHHHH!” In exactly two seconds, three things happened: 1) Aria's became completely rigid and her whole body shook, her mouth was frozen agape and her tonsils rattled with the most blood-curdling scream of pain imaginable as electricity coursed through her, setting every nerve-ending ablaze with agony; 2) In the stands, mothers held their terrified children's faces to their bosom, fathers and sons surged towards the barricades hurling obscenities and trash, barely held back by Rex Silver's already over-worked and exhausted security; 3) Rud Albion charged towards the Atheist Militia with his sledgehammer in the air like it was the fucking Hammer of the Gods, ready to bring down some holy righteous justice on ANY MOTHERFUCKER that DARED to stand in his way. The first two Militia met him near the top of the entrance ramp, charging into him with their shields held close to their bodies and their truncheons out at their sides. The Bronze Lion, screaming himself hoarse with fury, swung his sledgehammer in a wide arc, slamming into one of their shields and causing the bearer to lose his balance and topple to the ground. The second managed to get off a shot to the side of the Kingdom's Keeper's neck with his truncheon, but Rud, driven by pure adrenaline and vengeful bloodlust, NO SOLD and swung his sledgehammer again, crashing it into the Militia's shield as two more ran up the ramp towards him. The first got back up and came at Rud from behind, his truncheon high in the air... And Denton, still smiling and laughing, turned the dial again. “AAAAAAAIIIIIIIIIEEEEEEERRRRGGGGHHHHHH!” Again, Aria Murphy was shocked to her bones. Her eyes streamed with black mascara-tinted tears, saliva streaked her chin and her breathing became sharp, fast and painful. “RAAAARRRGGGHH!” Albion roared, spinning his sledgehammer 360 degrees and knocking over two of the Militia. Another truncheon shot blasted him in thigh and he dropped to one knee, but didn't pause for a second, swinging the hammer up uppercut-style and sending one of the Militia Men's helmet flying off his head. Another truncheon shot caught Rud straight on his ear, and he screamed through the pain: “AAAARRRRIIIAAAAA!” The Militia backed off from the blindly swinging Albion, deciding that he couldn't be contained with hand-to-hand weapons alone... the two machine-gun-sporting Militia cocked their weapons and trained them on Rud as he lumbered, dragging his sledgehammer, eyes wild... “Rud, when will you learn? Your supposedly 'good' qualities, the qualities that these piss-ants in the stands worship you for... your kindness, valour, honour and emotion... are but detriments. Time and time again, you face impossible odds, driven by such redundant concepts as hope, as love, as loyalty. And time and time again... you are manipulated, you are over-whelmed, and you are DESTROYED.” He turned the dial. “AAAAAAAIIIIIIIIIEEEEEEERRRRGGGGHHHHHH!” Aria's hair stood on end and she went into such severe spasms that she fell off the chair and landed in a tortured foetal position. Rud, knowing that he couldn't make it past the guards in the back of his mind, was driven by his love of the Pack's Lioness to run forward nonetheless, his sledgehammer poised and ready to - BOOM! The fans stopped breathing. Smoke curled out of the barrel of the Atheist Militia-man's machine-gun. Rud Albion had been blasted back a couple of feet and knocked flat on his back by the force of a rubber bullet to the ribs. His mouth opened and closed soundlessly as he tried to force air into his lungs. The fans were in silent shock. Rud was coughing and choking, spluttering painfully. But slowly, gradually, he began to move his fingers... then his arms... and began, SOMEHOW, to get back up. “I'M GIVING YOU THE CHOICE TO WALK AWAY, RUD,” Denton shouted. “WALK AWAY. THIS WOMAN IS NOTHING TO YOU. SHE IS A HINDRANCE, A BURDEN...AN IMPEDIMENT TO YOUR SUCCESS...” Rud got to his feet, steadying himself with one hand on the ground, his feral growl vibrating through his whole pain-racked body... Denton shook his head disapprovingly, and turned the dial. “AAAAAAAIIIIIIIIIEEEEEEERRRRGGGGHHHHHH!” As Aria writhed on the canvas, her heart on the verge of stopping, Rud lifted himself up as if possessed by some God of old and staggered further down the ramp, determined to fight his way past the Militia... BOOM! ...and again was thrown onto his back by another rubber bullet to the chest. Already, two huge blue bruises were appearing beneath his costume where the force of the short-rage shots had impacted against tender flesh. He tried with all of his will to force his legs to move, to fight the pain... but he couldn't. The Militia backed off as EMTs ran down from the back, stooping beside the fallen British Hero and feeling for his pulse. One of them reached inside his medical bag and pulled out a shot of morphine, loading it into a syringe. The fans were still in silent horror, mortified by the sickening scene that had unfolded before them. In the ring, Denton dropped down next to Aria, crawled over her body like some sort of snake, and stopped when he was face-to-face with her to look coldly into her eyes. Arturo handed him the microphone. “DO YOU SEE? For his LOVE, Rud Albion faced impossible odds, certain defeat. For his LOVE, he took two rubber bullets to the chest, taking years off his career. For LOVE of this pathetic weakling, he risked it all... and LOST. LOVE IS WEAKNESS.” The fans had already drenched the ring-side mats with soda and beer, pitched dozens of crushed cans and cups in and around the ring, and worked themselves into a sweat screaming their disdain for the Lizard. But when he planted a wet kiss on the mascara-stained, deathly pale cheek of Aria Murphy, they still found the energy to erupt into a chant to express their distaste: “FIRE DEAN DENTON!” clap-clap-clapclapclap “FIRE DEAN DENTON!” clap-clap-clapclapclap “FIRE DEAN DENTON!” clap-clap-clapclapclap Denton stood and stepped away from the tortured husk of Aria, allowing the apprehensive EMTs to slide into the ring and check on her condition. “I will see you at EndGame, Lady-HURPS and Gentle-DERPS, where the grand unveiling of my final, ULTIMATE masterpiece will take place. Until then... stay happy, stay healthy, and EAT WHAT YOU KILL.” Denton threw down the microphone and had his Militia escort he and Arturo to the back, stepping over the breathless body of Rud Albion on his way. He turned back and looked at the fallen Bronze Lion with a final sadistic smirk before disappearing behind the curtain. As the EMTs buzzed around the decimated duo, as Rud Albion slipped in and out of consciousness, only one thing was on his mind. Not salvation. But REVENGE. Toga Power...trip As always standing outside ready and prepped for interview was the lovely Jenna. She ran her fingers through her hair before lightly tapping on the door. It flung open and she was met with the large figure known as the Enforcer of PowerTrip. “Mr. Trip? Mr. Power?” she called past him into the room. The Enforcer stepped aside to let her in. “Oh my lord.” She gasped as her eyes focused in on the room before her. What would normally have been a board room for basketball meetings or whatever they do in this country, was now reminiscent of her college dorm days. Despite the fact that the men and women were all suited up in toga’s there were no chants and beer bongs going on. “Evening lovely,” greeted Mr. Power as stepped up to her with a smile and toga, and nothing between. “HI! Wow, so tonight is a Toga party? I used to do those a few years back. We always did these dares where the one that couldn’t drink all of the bong would have to strip off their Toga and parade around for the other’s amusements.” She sighed and kicked her heel. “For some reason I always lost. I think it was rigged.” “You never know doll.” “It was I am sure. I once caught Johnny with another bottle over the top of the bong as I was gorging myself. In fact I know they cheated. Those bastards! One of these days-“ “All in the past babe. Today is the present. Let us talk about now. Such as why we are graced with your beauty tonight?” “Right.” She adjusted herself and went all professional. “Well guys, you lost last week. The EndGame for you happened before you could even get started. What now?” “Sweet, if you weren’t so adorably cute I would be quite astonished and angered at such a retort for an opening question. But- you are right. We lost. Which may leave you to wonder why we are partying all Roman style? Truth is, there is no reason. When PowerTrip is around the party just happens. Unlike those Ravers that hold the prize that is. These two lovely ladies are twin sisters that enjoy the Italian lifestyle to its fullest. Don’t you dears?” They nodded and kissed him on both cheeks. “And I do mean full lifestyle Jenna.” He smiled as he pulled back and the two locked lips with each other. With a chuckle he tapped them to the side towards the couch while reproaching Jenna. “Some women… Incredible if you ask me. Back in the US they would call it incest, but up here it’s just two women enjoying themselves. Go figure. Viva la Italy.” “Right so… can we do this interview? I don’t wish to be rude but that is getting me rather-“ “Hot and bothered? Passionate? Moist?” He smirked as she blushed at the last remark: “Awkward, Mr. Power. Awkward. Rumors of what?” “Your match silly. Rome’s already talking about the headlines one of which has their utmost attention.” “Match? We’re out of the Four Ways to Glory slimdig. We have no match other then the EndGame itself right now. I think you have your-“ “Excuse me.” From behind the two at the door stood a lone worker. By his outfit it would appear that he was a delivery man of some sort. “Is there a Mr. Power or Mr. Trip here? I have a package that requires their signatures for delivery.” Power walked past Jenna and up to The Enforcer whom pleaded with his eyes through the mask to squish the puny morsel before him. Power shook his head and grabbed the clipboard which he signed. “You got my signature pal. Now what is this package?” He was handed a manila envelope which he quickly opened and pulled out a stack of papers. His eyes glanced across the contents. “Seriously? Who thought this was a good idea? Who booked it? Who do you work for kid?” he grabbed the worker by the collar but then released him and watched as he scurried back down the hall. His attention back to the papers in hand. Jenna walked up to him and asked him what was in the package. “Well?” Yeah that’s how she asked. Great businesswoman! “Your rumors may have been true dollface.” He sighed and smirked. “But it will NOT happen.” He pushed the papers back in the envelope and tossed it towards the table behind him. “Trip, let’s go. We need to have a sit-down with Mr. Hunt and SilverHAWK.” Mr. Trip groaned as he got up from the pile of bodies around him and made his way towards the doorway. “And girls- Daffeny, Tamara, Serena, and Tammy; come with. We’ll make sure this doesn’t go down.” The two left the room followed by four of the women, the twins stopped and gave Jenna a goodbye kiss as they passed her. Flustered Jenna stepped back a few steps to catch herself and grasped the table. She realized her hand was clutched over the folder and retrieved it. She pulled the paper up and her eyes lit up like a little child on Christmas morning, filled with happiness and joy. She dropped the papers back on the table and bounced happily out of the room as the camera remained behind to get a good close-up of the papers. On the top of the stack were the two that made up of the duo of the PowerTrip, and below their figures were the ominous words fighters everywhere await and dread at the same time. Versus. While in this case those words came with two equally powerful words that ACW had grown accustomed to. Two that were do some payback for what had happened to them. And it would happen at EndGame. It was signed right there on the flier. C O M M E R C I A L S A Toast To Bad Taste A lone, flickering candle illuminated what looked to be an extremely small room, in which sat a relatively average looking table and chair. We say chair because while there was indeed another chair -- for chairs not in pairs are bad luck, duh -- it was far from average. The Amazing Gabriel sat in it with his feat up on the table, the soles of his shoes covered in grime and dried, flattened gum. He was wearing his favorite pair. They’d traveled with him all around the world and stepped in vomit at all the best clubs. They’d danced with models and stomped on “the haters”. They had lived The Night Life just as long as anyone and their tapping indicated a slight impatience. He flicked his lighter and so allowed another small glimpse of the scene, bringing it upon his face to light a cigarette. His eyes were covered by his trademark shades, in spite of the room’s general lack of light. They say, whoever they may be, that the Sun never sets on a bad ass. “The Amazing One” lived this. Suddenly, he heard a faint knock at the door. He jumped to his feet, a feat that surprised him given the party favors he had taken only a half hour earlier. “Password?” He brought his ear close to the door, waiting to hear a response that would either have him letting the person outside in with open arms or beating them close to a pulp. A whisper came from the hallway. “Pendulum.” Gabriel opened the door swiftly and exclaimed, “CHESTER!” He greeted the rotund man dressed in all white with a fist pound and chest bump, pleasantries that the man just couldn’t grasp. It was a generational thing, the same kind that prohibited him from understanding why The Amazing Gabriel’s secret password was Pendulum. They exchanged wide, cheesing smiles, as if competing to see whose could look most forced. Gabriel extended his arms in the direction of the table. “Sit!” They took their respective seats at the table and sat in silence for a moment before Gabriel’s voice once again broke it. “Well, did you bring it or what?!” Chester laughed heartily, reaching into the liner pocket of his overcoat. “Oh, Gabe. You young kids are such lushes these days. Of course I brought it, but I warn you. This is what you might call the good shit, four times as expensive as the most beautiful whore in Italy, so take it easy...” He set a flask on the table, which “The Amazing One” eyed with ferocity. He licked his lips as the agent set down two small shot glasses shortly thereafter. “Pish posh!” The Amazing Gabriel snorted, snatching the flask before Wallace could blink and taking a passionate rip from it. “Christ Almighty, where are your manners?” Wallace scolded. “We haven’t even made a proper toast!” “Fine, get on with it then!” responded Gabriel defiantly. As Chester began to pour the shot glasses full of dark, aged brandy, Tyson XL was standing twenty feet away in the hallway. He had been looking for The Amazing Gabriel for what to him felt like ages. He was feeling good and didn’t want to wait any longer to hit the town. A member of the ACW road crew had told him they’d seen “The Amazing One” come this way about a half hour ago... He walked the hallway slowly. It was just a little too eerie, even for him. Finally, he saw a faint flicker. It was coming through a little crack in a door. Curiosity getting the best of him, he drew closer to the door and as he did the Southern boom grew louder. “The road that led me here to All-Star Championship Wrestling has indeed been a tumultuous one, but I’m proud to say that it’s taken a pit stop here at this table. It’s hard to be trusted with your own well being, let alone that of another, but I’m glad that those we handle are so easily prodded. So here is to homemade enemies and the best laid plans!” The Amazing Gabriel’s voice added on, “And to fat checks!” “Here, here!” they shouted resoundingly into the air. Even if he hadn’t been standing right outside the door, Tyson would’ve heard them for miles. He wondered if his ears could be deceiving him, although deep down knew they weren’t. Backing into the wall, he clenched his fists, the clanking and then slamming of glass echoing in the hall around him. His stomach lurched. “Oops, must have been the brandy!” chortled Wallace. He had experienced a lurching of the stomach as well. As the celebration continued, Oso Bannon sat alone and unawaree in his dressing room clutching a handful of some party favors of his own. Shoveling the assorted blend of pills into his mouth, he leaned his head back against a locker and drifted away once more into his music, satisfied with the night and what was to come. Pink Floyd had given way to Radiohead, their song “The Bends” reaching its slowed, somber end. Where do we go from here? The words are coming out all weird. Where are you now when I need you? the start of the resistance The water fountain across the street from the Land Rover Arena wasn't particularly spectacular. It had some cultural significance, but to Rory Hayes, all it represented was a meeting point. Pulling up the zipper of his jacket, Rory suddenly heard footsteps behind him. Funnily enough, he smiled. Right on time, he thought. There was a sudden gust of wind. Hayes thought it was fitting. "Never thought this would happen. Not like this. At least the wheelchair's nice." John Sarsgaard cleared his throat, stepping out of the shadows "Stranger things have happened." It came to pass that only two men weren't engaging in fisticuffs. Well, three. Y'know, SilverHAWK being knocked out and all? Yeah. The two in question? Jeremy Hunt and Z. "I'm surprised you did all of this." Hunt spoke, unable to find the words at first. Z shrugged his shoulders. "It was your new boy Denton there who had the idea of locking everybody out. It was YOUR plan to see this come to fruition. I just agreed to be the one who kicked things into motion. It's what I do, natch. I'm just as surprised as you are that we were able to see eye-to-eye to pull this off together. Perhaps there is a way we CAN co-exist. Who would have thunk it, huh? Jeremy Hunt and Z, on opposite ends of the spectrum banding together to bring about the death of ACW and usher in its spiritual rebirth? No wonder Old Man HAWK there never saw it coming." Well. There you have it. Renaud Cardinal heard it all, and went ballistic. He again let loose with a string of vulgarities, unable to contain his disdain for what was unfurling in front of his very eyes. It came to the point where Renaud tore his headset off and tossed it down to the ground. Unfortunately for Cardinal, he didn't have time to roll his sleeves all the way up. Why's that? He got punched in the face, that's why. Z turned around and chuckled loudly at Cardinal laying on the ground, a peach of a shiner forming on the side of his French-Canadian face. Jeremy Hunt gasped once more. Mainly because the person that punched Renaud... was his own broadcast partner, 'The One' Jimmy Reid. Jimmy stood up and snarled, drawing a ton of heat for what he just did. "Well, Mr. Reid; has everything been to your satisfaction so far?" the Masked Enigma asked. Reid walked over to Z, gave Hunt the once-over, and then laughed. "Yeah, pretty much what I expected. None of those idiots out there are even paying attention to us." He was dead right. The disorder that was unfolding was rather frightening. Everybody was fight everybody. A couple of the key battles? Rud Albion and Dean Denton. Arkady Rasputin and God's Forgotten Son. Omega and Andy Sharp. Keith Scott Zimmerman versus everybody in sight (mainly because Keller had chosen to run for his life), and clearing a path for GoldenHAWK to climb into the ring. Sheffield versus Fejona, and Iceman, and Malk Al-Haq, and Kati. It was more of Sheffield getting his arse kicked by people not named Fejona, who had wisely opted to head for the hills. Meanwhile, inside the ring, GoldenHAWK finally got to his downed father. SilverHAWK hadn't moved since getting kicked in the head. G-HAWK began to panic, and cradled his father's half-conscious body in his arms. KSZ's valiant efforts to clear a path for the EMTs worked, and a stream of them raced down the ramp. "GET IN HERE~!" G-HAWK screamed, holding back his tears. "HE'S STOPPED BREATHING! PLEASE!" ... Holy... shit. Jeremy Hunt swallowed hard. Powerful stuff. "So. We're calling this a truce?" Jeremy asked of Z. Z began to shuffle towards the security barricade, where Fejona Min had cleared a path for him and Jimmy Reid. "Not really a truce. More of... an understanding?" Hunt's throat went dry again. He took another glance at the mayhem in the ring and around the ring. It was all his doing. But the carnage that had just broken out; the man from SlySports hadn't quite expected just how out of control it had gotten. He sure as hell didn't count on SilverHAWK being beaten to a pulp. He turned back to Z and nodded without convinction. "Okay. An understanding it is." Z smirked, then peered over his shoulder at Jimmy Reid. A twisted half-smile on the face of the former ACW World Champion was enough for the Masked Enigma to set his eyes on a nearby camera. His next words sent chills down the spines of everybody. Hell, even Hunt himself was a bit taken aback and his knees wobbled a little. The Caped Crusader? Oh, how he cherished this particular moment in time: "Two minutes." Ladies and gentlemen. See you at the end of the year. See you at END GAME 2010. See you... at midnight. WINNERS: ... F I N.
